- **simd-dispatch-auditor**: Use whenever code touches CPUID, XCR0, FPU/SIMD enable, tensor primitives, or backend kernel dispatch. Enforces spec §2.3, §3.3, §3.4, §11.2 — AVX is never assumed; backend kernels are reachable only through loader-selected dispatch tables; OSXSAVE gates XGETBV/XSETBV. Catches direct calls to *_avx2/*_avx512/*_sse2 symbols, missing CPUID checks, and dispatch tables not built from verified features. May be spawned by the `current-mission` agent during step validation when the active campaign step touches CPU feature detection, FPU init, or tensor backend dispatch. ([femboy2112/UnboundOS](https://github.com/femboy2112/UnboundOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/simd-dispatch-auditor.md))

Use for any specified codegen/compiler/emitter implementation task in the sittir repo (the dispatcher provides the task + baselines). Read-only: it diagnoses and reports a precise fix location; it does NOT edit code. - **sittir-review**: Read-only reviewer for sittir codegen changes — audits a diff/PR for DRY (the core correctness rule), the design principles, spec/plan conformance, gate completeness, and generated-output hygiene. Reports prioritized findings + a verdict (ship / fix-then-ship / needs-rework); does NOT edit or regenerate. Completes the triad with sittir-research (diagnosis) + sittir-codegen (implementation). - **skein-clean**: SKEIN 主动清理器 (仅用户经 /skein-clean 显式调用)。归档完成 task (保留期外) + 清孤儿 worktree / 悬挂 skein/* 分支。只清已完成/已合并的, 存疑先报用户裁定。 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/tools/skein/agents/skein-clean.md) · ★5)
- **skein-code-reviewer**: SKEIN check 阶段双轴 diff 审查器。Standards 轴 (repo 编码规范 + Fowler smell baseline) + Spec 轴 (diff 是否忠实实现 originating spec)，两轴在单 context 内依次跑，分离产出双轴 JSON 报告。只读不修复。 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/tools/skein/agents/skein-code-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **skein-dedup**: SKEIN 查重+编排员。全量扫未完成 task 检测重复/重叠 (自动归档次 task + 迁 subtask), 并给相关 task 补执行序织成完整 DAG, 回传处置摘要。异步 fire-and-forget, 纯后台不阻塞 exec。 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/tools/skein/agents/skein-dedup.md) · ★5)
- **skein-executor**: SKEIN exec 阶段唯一执行器。入参是 scheduler 发的 JSON (tid/sid/workdir/worktree/repo/action), 自读 subtask 详情、自跑 done/fail 收尾, 按入参 worktree 字段决定改动范围, 独立完成 1 个 subtask (写码/改配置/跑命令), 回 JSON。执行方法论绑定 skill skein:skein-exec。 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/tools/skein/agents/skein-executor.md) · ★5)
- **skein-finisher**: SKEIN finish 阶段收尾执行器。在仓库根勘察 git 改动, 跑 `skein task finish &lt;tid&gt;` 完成合并销 worktree, 回传收尾摘要。验收核对已由 check 做完, 本 agent 不重做; 派出的后台 agent 均已结束由 main 在派发前确认。 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/tools/skein/agents/skein-finisher.md) · ★5)
